Lomalistat - Third-Party Notices
===============================

Snapshot date: 2026-10-27
Dependency source: package-lock.json (lockfileVersion 3)
Lockfile SHA-256: 72fe2dd96c03aece477fbf318dac6a784868967581d9630ba0ea09c50c88b4fb

The project's own code remains: All rights reserved. Internal tool.
This file does not grant rights to the project's own code or replace the
licenses of any third-party components.

Scope
-----
This file covers the installed packages marked as non-development dependencies
in the lockfile, all top-level Workbox packages (including generated service
worker/runtime components), and all direct development dependencies listed in
package.json. This is a conservative inventory: optional Workbox features and
development tools are not necessarily included in the browser application.

Package-level license and notice files are reproduced below, including their
own bundled-dependency notices. Additional source-level attributions for
pdf-lib, @pdf-lib/standard-fonts and pako are preserved with those packages.
Line endings and trailing whitespace have been normalized for this text file.

This is not a complete inventory of development-only transitive packages or
uninstalled optional platform binaries. When redistributing development tools
or node_modules, retain those packages' own license and copyright files too.

Include this file with redistributed copies of the application. For a compiled
browser distribution, copy it from the project root into dist after building
and distribute it alongside index.html. Refresh this file when the locked
dependencies change, using the license/notice files of the exact new versions.

--- pako/lib/zlib/README (zlib license and attributions) ---

Content of this folder follows zlib C sources as close as possible.
That's intended to simplify maintainability and guarantee equal API
and result.

Key differences:

- Everything is in JavaScript.
- No platform-dependent blocks.
- Some things like crc32 rewritten to keep size small and make JIT
  work better.
- Some code is different due missed features in JS (macros, pointers,
  structures, header files)
- Specific API methods are not implemented (see notes in root readme)

This port is based on zlib 1.2.8.

This port is under zlib license (see below) with contribution and addition of javascript
port under expat license (see LICENSE at root of project)

Copyright:
(C) 1995-2013 Jean-loup Gailly and Mark Adler
(C) 2014-2017 Vitaly Puzrin and Andrey Tupitsin

=============================================================================

  people who reported problems and suggested various improvements in zlib; they
  are too numerous to cite here.

## is-glob@v4.0.3

> Returns `true` if the given string looks like a glob pattern or an extglob pattern. This makes it easy to create code that only uses external modules like node-glob when necessary, resulting in much faster code execution and initialization time, and a better user experience.

This requirement includes Essential Claims that I own and any that I have the right to license without obligation of payment or other consideration to an unrelated third party. If I do not join the Corresponding Working Group, I agree to offer patent licenses according to the W3C Royalty-Free licensing requirements described in Section 5 of the W3C Patent Policy for the portions of the Specification included in the resulting Recommendation. 7.2.2. No Disclosure Obligation. If I do not join the Corresponding Working Group, I have no patent disclosure obligations outside of those set forth in Section 6 of the W3C Patent Policy.
